- 当前独有正文：Custom CNC parts Made to your drawing Custom milling, turning and 5-axis machining for prototypes and small-batch production. Prepare RFQ email Browse part examples Machining concept · AI illustration Find a familiar part form Source examples for discussing your component. All part examples Machined impeller Curved blades and a central hub bore. Casting housing Ribs, cavities and machined interfaces. Turned locking pin Stepped diameters and a threaded mounting form. Plastic components Repeated holes and thin connecting sections. Choose a process or material The geometry, stock and acceptance requirements determine the machining route. Machining capabilities CNC milling CNC turning 5-axis machining Plastic machining Machining of castings Materials Aluminum Stainless steel Brass & copper Engineering plastics Specify the exact grade, stock condition and finish in your request. Starting with a physical part? See how a sample becomes an agreed manufacturing definition. A machining partner in Hangzhou Company information and original workshop records for your supplier review. Factory & quality Founded 2017 Production facility 1,500 m² Machining & supporting equipment 40+ Machining centers in the LZZ workshop · original photograph Archived CMM equipment photograph Agree how the part will be accepted Critical dimensions, sampling and inspection records are confirmed for your order. The equipment photograph is not an inspection result or calibration record. LZZ is ISO 9001 certified. Request the current certificate and scope. Review inspection planning See the setup behind the part Original machining photographs with a focused explanation of the work. Machining studies CNC machining a 29-inch display housing Supporting a wide panel while machining its mounting features. Turning followed by milling Keeping the turned axis and secondary milled features related. Have a drawing to review? Prepare the drawing and revision, material and finish, quantity, critical features and delivery destination. Orders start from 1 piece. The reference production window is 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including surface finishing. Shipping time is additional. Use the RFQ checklist Prepare RFQ email The website prepares a draft. Attach your drawings and send it from your email app.

- 当前独有正文：Home / About LZZ Custom machining in Hangzhou. LZZ Precision · Hangzhou Lizhizao Precision Machinery Co., Ltd. We make custom CNC machined parts to your drawing. Get to know the company, review how an order moves from quotation to delivery, and find the information needed for supplier qualification. Founded in Hangzhou 2017 Production facility 1,500 m² Machining & supporting equipment 40+ Read the company profile LZZ machining workshop · Hangzhou, China Choose the company information you need Company profile Legal identity, workshop, engineering experience and original company recognition documents. How we work Drawing review, quotation scope, sample approval, inspection and delivery responsibilities. Company news Dated records and original photographs from workshop visits, events and team activities. Contact LZZ Email, WhatsApp, the Hangzhou address and arrangements for a workshop visit. Qualification and part acceptance Company qualification, the requirements on your drawing and the agreed order each need their own review. Company and quality system LZZ is ISO 9001 certified. Request the current certificate, holder and scope. Enterprise recognition and teaching records have separate purposes. Review supplier documents Drawing and acceptance Define the revision, material, critical features, finish and inspection records needed to accept your part. Prepare the part requirements Order and delivery Agree quantity, quoted work, production schedule, packing and shipping responsibilities for the order. Review the order process Dated company records Workshop visits and company events provide a closer look at the people and setting. 7 January 2025 An Australian customer visits the LZZ workshop Looking at a component on the workshop floor · original photograph An Australian customer visited LZZ in Hangzhou for a look around the CNC workshop and a conversation about machined parts. 19 November 2025 LZZ at the China-Africa West Lake Summit LZZ representatives at the China-Africa West Lake Summit backdrop · original photograph LZZ representatives attended the China-Africa West Lake Summit in Hangzhou, with a company display introducing its custom machining services. Browse company news Start with your drawing Prepare the drawing revision, material and quantity so we can review your part requirements. The website prepares an email draft. Review it, attach drawings and send it from your email app. Prepare RFQ email Check the RFQ inputs

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：profile-mobile.png、profile-desktop.png。
- 当前H1：LZZ Precision in Hangzhou
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Engineering scope for your component → h3: Laboratory & scientific instruments → h3: Automotive & motorcycle prototypes → h3: Robotics & automation → h3: Electronic & audio equipment → h3: Hydraulic & rescue equipment → h3: Agricultural machinery → h3: Industrial fans & impellers → h3: Aerospace & defense equipment → h3: Semiconductor equipment → h3: UAVs, 3D printers & motion systems → h3: Medical devices & industrial equipment → h2: Company recognition & supplier documents → h3: High-tech enterprise certificate → h3: Zhejiang science & technology SME → h3: Precision manufacturing teaching base → h3: ISO 9001 quality-system certificate → h2: Company development → h3: Founded in 2017 → h3: Expanded machining capacity → h3: First-article and production inspection → h3: Additional inspection equipment → h3: Overseas customers → h2: People and workshop visits → h3: A workshop visit → h3: An evening with the team → h2: Choose the next supplier-review step
- 当前独有正文：Home / About LZZ / Company profile LZZ Precision in Hangzhou Hangzhou Lizhizao Precision Machinery Co., Ltd. We make custom CNC machined components to your drawing, from an initial part to repeat production. Milling, turning and multi-axis work are reviewed against the material and acceptance requirements. Founded in Hangzhou 2017 Production facility 1,500 m² Machining & supporting equipment 40+ Review company recognition & documents Machining centers in the LZZ workshop · Hangzhou Engineering scope for your component Our engineering team has more than 15 years of CNC machining experience. We review functional surfaces, locating datums and tool access before agreeing the machining and inspection scope. Parts may combine pockets and mounting features with bores, shoulders or threads. The model and controlled drawing determine the route and dimensions to check. Machining capabilities Inspection planning An aisle through the LZZ production floor · original photograph These application families help frame a component discussion. Your drawing and project requirements define machining, traceability and approval. Laboratory & scientific instruments Instrument blocks, brackets and housings with mounting interfaces, bores and internal passages. Explore instrument components Automotive & motorcycle prototypes Billet covers, intake components and throttle housings for developing designs and checking assemblies. Explore automotive prototypes Robotics & automation Mounting plates, sensor brackets and compact housings reviewed against assembly interfaces and access requirements. Explore multi-axis machining Electronic & audio equipment Enclosures, mounting structures and precision housings, with attention to fit and visible surfaces. Explore aluminum housings Other application families to discuss Company recognition & supplier documents These original records cover enterprise recognition and a teaching partnership. The ISO 9001 quality-system certificate serves a separate purpose. Enterprise recognition High-tech enterprise certificate Issued to Hangzhou Lizhizao Precision Machinery Co., Ltd., with seals from the Zhejiang science and technology, finance and tax authorities. Printed date / validity Issued 8 December 2023 · printed validity: three years What it covers High-tech enterprise recognition Provincial recognition Zhejiang science & technology SME The document identifies Lizhizao as a Zhejiang science and technology SME and names the provincial science and technology authority. Printed date / validity No issue date stated on the displayed document What it covers Provincial enterprise recognition Teaching partnership Precision manufacturing teaching base The plaque names Zhejiang Institute of Mechanical & Electrical Engineering and Lizhizao as a practical teaching base. Printed date / validity November 2023 What it covers Education collaboration Dates and validity describe the displayed originals. Request the current status for supplier qualification. ISO 9001 quality-system certificate LZZ is ISO 9001 certified. Request the current certificate, holder, scope and validity for your supplier review. Request the current ISO 9001 certificate Company qualification and part acceptance need different evidence. Agree material traceability, critical dimensions, sampling, final surface condition and project-specific records in the order. Review order documentation. Company development Founded in 2017 LZZ began making precision CNC machined parts for domestic equipment manufacturers. Expanded machining capacity We added milling and turning equipment, including multi-axis machines, for a wider range of industrial parts. First-article and production inspection We introduced first-article checks and inspections during production and before shipment. Additional inspection equipment A CMM, height gauges and optical projectors expanded our dimensional inspection capabilities. Overseas customers We expanded our OEM/ODM machining services to customers in Asia, North America and Europe. People and workshop visits Original company records show visits to the workshop and time outside it with the team. A component discussion on the workshop floor · January 2025 visit record A workshop visit The visit record includes a component discussion and a tour of the production floor. An evening group photograph · May 2025 team outing An evening with the team The May 2025 outing archive records an outdoor meal and evening group photographs. Choose the next supplier-review step Have a drawing ready? Prepare its revision, material, quantity and acceptance requirements. For company qualification, request the current documents directly. The website prepares an email draft. Review it, attach drawings and send it from your email app. Prepare RFQ email Request supplier documents

## /about/how-we-work/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：workflow-mobile.png、workflow-desktop.png。
- 当前H1：From drawing review to finished parts.
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Review the drawing and complete quotation → h2: Approve a defined sample → h2: Release production and agree inspection → h2: Protect the parts and agree delivery → h2: Keep changes and discrepancy decisions clear → h3: Changes after approval → h3: Nonconforming parts and after-sales terms → h2: Start with controlled requirements
- 当前独有正文：Home / About LZZ / How we work From drawing review to finished parts. Keep a controlled revision and acceptance plan from the first quotation through sample approval, production and handover. Confirm the commercial terms for the specific order. Minimum order From 1 piece Production 7–14 business days After drawing and order confirmation, including surface finishing. Transit time is additional. Payment and samples Agreed in the quotation Confirm payment stages, sample machining, setup and freight charges, and any later-order credit. Drawing review Quotation Sample approval Production Handover Prepare the RFQ inputs Review the drawing and complete quotation Confirm what controls the part, what the price covers and which decisions remain open. Buyer Supply the controlled drawing and model with part number, units and revision. State material, quantity, part function and required arrival date. For a starting sample, identify what to retain or change and the measurement and drawing work needed. LZZ Review geometry, workholding, tool access and critical features. Raise questions and propose a machining route or alternatives for review. State the process, material, finish, quantity, inspection and records, production schedule and shipping basis in the quotation. Agree Identify the controlling file if model and drawing disagree. Approve proposed geometry or setup changes and permitted alternatives before production. Identify tooling, special documentation, freight and duties where charged separately. Confirm payment method, currency, payment stages and the amount at each stage. For samples, agree machining, setup and freight charges, including whether a sample fee can be credited toward a later order. Compare quotation scope Approve a defined sample Agree the evaluation before machining. Record exactly which configuration and requirements the decision covers. Buyer Define whether the sample is for fit, appearance or a defined functional test. Identify who supplies mating components, fixtures and procedures, who checks it and the actual approver. LZZ Make and review the sample against the agreed drawing revision, material, finish and evaluation scope. Record any approved alternatives or unresolved differences. Agree Record first-article results, the configuration evaluated, fit/appearance/function checks and release criteria. Prototype material or finish differences limit what the sample can establish. A revised design or changed material, finish or route may require sample approval again. Release the production batch as a separate decision. Machined prototype approval checklist Release production and agree inspection Define the measurements, sampling and records in the quotation or order. This is not a default promise of 100% inspection. Item Agree for the batch Released configuration Use the approved drawing revision, material and finish. Re-review changes before machining the revised part. Sampling and critical features Agree first-article checks, sampling and the dimensions and datums needing recorded measurements. Inspection scope is agreed for the order. Measurement and evidence Confirm method, instrument range, available calibration evidence and report format before ordering. A CMM photograph does not establish current calibration or part results. Records and discrepancies Agree material traceability, record references, delivery documentation and handling of nonconforming parts before the batch starts. Inspection planning Order documentation LZZ coordinate measuring machine · methods, calibration evidence and reports are confirmed for the order. Protect the parts and agree delivery Agree protection, identification and handover responsibilities alongside the production schedule. Protected components in the LZZ packing archive · November 2022. Historical preparation; packing is agreed for each order. Buyer Provide destination, receiving contact, required arrival date and any handling limits. Identify cosmetic faces, seals, threads, fragile edges, cleanliness or corrosion-protection needs and labels. LZZ Provide protective packing, a packing list and commercial invoice, plus shipment information. Include material, finishing and dimensional records when agreed in the order. Agree Select cartons or wooden cases, separation, pack weight and handling. Match part number, revision, batch and quantity to labels and the packing list. Distinguish dispatch from arrival. Agree transport, Incoterm and version, named place, booking, customs clearance, duties, taxes, insurance and local delivery responsibilities. Courier, air or sea depends on size and destination; DHL, FedEx or UPS can be discussed. EXW, FOB or DDP applies as quoted. DDP is not automatic. Confirm destination-specific documents before production. Packing and international shipping checklist Keep changes and discrepancy decisions clear Changes after approval A drawing or quantity change can affect the quoted price, route and schedule. Review changed features and update the scope; a revised drawing may need sample approval again. Plan prototypes and repeat orders Nonconforming parts and after-sales terms Send the revision, part or batch reference, affected quantity, photographs and measurements. LZZ reviews the discrepancy against the agreed acceptance criteria and agrees corrective action with you. Confirm the warranty period and start date, exclusions and reporting procedure with the order. Agree responsibility for rework, replacement, return freight and any refund. Discuss a part discrepancy Start with controlled requirements Prepare the drawing revision, material and quantity, critical features and acceptance records. Mark the decisions that still need review. The website prepares an email draft. Review it, attach drawings and send it from your email app. Prepare RFQ email Check the RFQ inputs

## /capabilities/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：capabilities-mobile.png、capabilities-desktop.png。
- 当前H1：CNC machining capabilities
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Choose the route for the features → h2: Known material, precise specification → h3: Aluminum → h3: Stainless steel → h3: Copper & brass → h3: Engineering plastics → h2: Define the decision before the order → h3: Evaluate a prototype → h3: Release a pilot or repeat batch → h3: Start from an existing sample → h2: Look at the setup and the inspection plan → h3: Machining setup records → h3: Checks, approval and records → h2: Bring the part requirements together
- 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities CNC machining capabilities Start with the part requirements. Custom CNC machining for prototypes and small production runs. Choose a route from the features, material or information you have today. Choose by geometry Start with a material Sample, prototype or first release Have a drawing? Prepare RFQ email AI-generated part-family illustration. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Choose the route for the features These process illustrations explain the route. The model, working interfaces and setup constraints determine the proposed machining method. Machining route Part features Information to provide Review the route CNC milling AI process concept Pockets, flat faces and hole patterns in custom components. Datums, pocket depths, internal radii, stock and clamping faces. Explore CNC milling CNC turning AI process concept Shafts, sleeves, discs and concentric features around a shared axis. Datum axis, working diameters, complete threads and any off-axis features. Explore CNC turning 5-axis machining AI process concept Angled faces and continuous contours needing access from several directions. Current model, finished and stock envelope, weight and fixture clearance. Explore 5-axis machining Plastic machining AI process concept Polymer forms with support and measurement conditions reviewed together. Exact grade, fillers, working environment and conditioning. Also listed under materials. Explore plastic machining Machining of castings AI process concept Finished bores, faces and datums machined into a cast blank. Blank and finished drawings, allowance, supply and rejected-blank responsibility. Explore machining of castings Known material, precise specification The drawing defines the grade, condition and finish. A thumbnail shows a part form; it does not identify the material. Aluminum Specify alloy, temper, stock form and the finish; identify masked fits and final dimensions. Review material requirements Source photo Stainless steel Select the exact grade and condition for the service environment and treatment. Review material requirements Source photo Copper & brass Separate contact, heat-transfer and fluid-sealing requirements; specify the alloy standard. Review material requirements Source photo Engineering plastics Specify polymer grade and fillers with loads, temperature, moisture and inspection state. Review material requirements Source photo Define the decision before the order A prototype, repeat release and existing sample start from different inputs and approval points. Evaluate a prototype Purpose Answer a fit, assembly, appearance or defined functional question. Drawing status Current revision, with fixed interfaces and open design decisions. Quantity information Quantity needed for the evaluation. Approval point Checks, records and the person authorized to approve the result. Plan this project stage Release a pilot or repeat batch Purpose Plan an order against an approved production baseline. Drawing status Approved drawing revision for each part number; changes authorized before release. Quantity information Quantity by part number and release; forecasts kept separate. Approval point First-article hold point, sampling, traceability and repeat requirements. Plan this project stage Start from an existing sample Purpose Establish the intended replacement and manufacturing definition. Drawing status Resolve wear, hidden geometry and unknown material before approving a model and drawing. Quantity information First-part quantity plus any expected repeat demand. Approval point Approve the definition, then fit / functional checks before repeat manufacture. Plan this project stage Review drawing, approval and delivery responsibilities Look at the setup and the inspection plan Original photographs support a supplier review within the visible scope of each record. Original first setup from a two-stage milling record. The workpiece, cutter and holding arrangement are visible. Machining setup records Review milling, turning and multi-axis setups. Photographs do not establish a new part's material, tolerances or delivery outcome. Review machining studies Original LZZ CMM photograph. Equipment presence does not establish current calibration, range or a completed report. Checks, approval and records Agree critical features, methods, sampling and approval responsibility for the drawing. Review factory and quality requirements Bring the part requirements together Current model and drawing with units and revision Material grade, condition, finish and protected surfaces Quantity, critical features and inspection records Delivery destination and requested date State any undecided material, process or design requirements in the request. Prepare RFQ email Prepare a draft in the browser, then review it, attach drawings and send it from your email application.

## /capabilities/5-axis-cnc-machining/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：无。
- 当前H1：5-Axis CNC Machining Services
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Fit the process to the drawing → h3: Match the tool path to the surface → h3: Size and workholding inputs → h3: Indexed positioning → h3: Simultaneous motion → h3: Read the blade, root and hub together → h2: Angled faces & complex contours → h3: Multi-bore circular milled component → h3: Turbine & fluid-system components → h3: Multi-sided machined components → h2: Material and finish review → h2: Five-axis machining in practice → h3: Review the rotary setup → h3: Relate the features across faces → h2: Scope to confirm separately → h3: Does machining include balance or application approval? → h2: Request a 5-axis machining quote
- 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / 5-axis machining 5-Axis CNC Machining Services Machining for angled faces and curved surfaces. CNC machining for angled features and continuous contours. Define the datums and tool access, with inspection requirements tied to the model revision. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Fit & acceptance Part forms Prepare RFQ email Fit the process to the drawing Angled features may be reached by indexed positioning; continuous contours may need a simultaneous tool path. We review the model, tool reach and fixture clearance before selecting the route. Size and workholding inputs Tool reach, fixture clearance and protected surfaces in each orientation. Send finished and stock dimensions, known weight and protected clamping areas. Machine travel, load, orientation and fixture limits are assessed per drawing. Indexed positioning Hold the rotary axes at a selected orientation to reach an angled face. Relate features across faces to the same datum scheme. Simultaneous motion Change orientation along a continuous contour. The model, tool reach and fixture clearance determine whether this route is needed. Review tool access and handling; agree balance, leak or other functional tests separately. Support the thin sections Stock dimensions, fixture support and stress relief for thin sections. Review stock removal and tool access around the final walls, pockets, bores and mounting datums. Finished enclosure forms from the milling source group; not an inspection result. Define the part after finishing Specify treatment standards and material compatibility. Mark masked fits, cosmetic faces and the dimensions that apply after blasting, brushing, polishing, anodizing, conversion coating or powder coating. Cast parts also need blank acceptance, locating targets and machining allowance. Identify the stock form in the request. Does an automotive prototype include vehicle testing? Specify pressure, leak, assembly and vehicle-performance tests separately. For fabricated or welded components, identify supplied items and joint requirements. Send the blank specification and machining allowance, with bearing bores and sealing faces defined on the finished-part drawing. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Fit & acceptance Part forms Prepare RFQ email Fit the process to the drawing The casting and finished-part drawings need to distinguish as-cast surfaces from areas to machine. Confirm blank supply and acceptance before we plan the machining work. Separate the blank from the finished interfaces Provide both casting and finished-part requirements. Confirm how the irregular blank and finished part are located for measurement and which finished-interface results are recorded. Sealing & hidden geometry Seal lands, sections for internal features and final surface requirements. Agree dimensional acceptance; porosity, pressure and non-destructive tests require separate criteria. Plan the checks and approval stages Cast blank and finish-machined interfaces. AI-generated comparison. Use the feature drawing to settle the route Milling, boring, drilling and tapping of cast housings and pump bodies. If drawings are incomplete, we can reconstruct the geometry from a sample. Identify critical features and permitted geometry changes. Machining establishes bearing bores, seal lands and mounting interfaces. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Provide material records and define who supplies the blanks. Blank condition Include casting dimensions and available machining allowance. Agree cleaning or preparation required before machining. Define incoming acceptance, variation and rejected-blank responsibility. Final treatment Specify treatment, masking and the inspection stage. Finish selected for the alloy and application. Identify final bores, seal faces and dimensions after treatment. Material and finish checklist From cast blank to inspected part Confirm the blank supply and acceptance before the machining plan is released. Review the casting Compare the blank specification with the finished drawing. Check allowance, locating areas and supply responsibility. Machine the working interfaces Plan milling, boring, drilling and tapping around the agreed datums and available allowance. Agree final acceptance Confirm dimensional records and any separate porosity or functional tests before production. Blank supply, allowance, locating targets and rejected-blank responsibility belong in the agreed manufacturing definition. Review reconstruction from an existing housing Scope to confirm separately Can you start from an existing housing? Sample reconstruction can be reviewed as a separate scope. Hidden features still require measurements or drawing details, and the model and acceptance requirements need approval before machining. Are pressure and porosity tests included? Specify those requirements separately, with the procedure and acceptance limits. A dimensional machining check does not establish pressure performance or casting integrity. - 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / CNC milling CNC Milling Services Flat faces, pockets and holes in custom parts. CNC milling for blocks, brackets and housings. Send your drawing with mating features, finish requirements and any limits on tool access. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Fit & acceptance Part forms Prepare RFQ email AI-generated milling concept. Fit the process to the drawing An instrument housing or bracket may need features machined from several directions. We review the model to choose the stock form and setup sequence, with tool access checked for each feature. Pockets, holes and shared datums Show pocket depths, internal radii, hole positions and the faces used to locate the part. Identify geometry that can change if a longer tool or an additional setup would otherwise be needed. Size and workholding inputs Part and stock dimensions, clamping faces and tool clearance. Usable size depends on the fixture. Feature / decision Specify on the drawing Agree before production Faces & mounting holes Assembly datums, mounting faces, hole positions, bore fits and relationships between faces. Agree measurement methods and the dimensions and positions to record against the specified datums. Pockets & thin walls Pocket depths, minimum walls, internal radii, permitted tool access and any allowed radius or geometry changes. Review distortion, tool marks and the limits required at critical sections. Finish & protected areas Finish, masking, cosmetic faces and dimensions that apply before or after treatment. Agree the acceptance stage and required finishing records. Define cleanliness, chemical-contact or ESD tests separately. Selected critical features: ±0.01 mm. This reference applies only to selected features after reviewing the material, geometry, setup and measurement method. Confirm each critical tolerance on the drawing; ±0.01 mm does not apply to every feature or part. Plan the checks and approval stages Typical milled features. AI-generated illustration. Use the feature drawing to settle the route Pockets, slots, faces and contours; 3-, 4- or 5-axis setups selected from the drawing. Identify critical features and permitted geometry changes. The concept explains the review; it is not an inspected part or setup record. Parts suited to CNC milling Blocks, brackets and housings combine flat faces, pockets and holes. Their datums and tool access determine the setup sequence. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Milled instrument blocks & brackets Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Features across several faces, with tool access from different directions. View part requirements Lid & gasket aluminum housings Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Housing pockets and bores with mounting interfaces. View part requirements Material and finish review Specify grade, condition, finish and the final acceptance state together. These are discussion options; suitability and availability are confirmed for the drawing. Material / scope Specification Finish / treatment Confirm for this drawing Aluminum Specify alloy and temper; examples include 6061 and 7075. Deburring, polishing, bead blasting or anodizing. Mark protected fits and dimensions that apply after treatment. Steel & stainless steel Specify the exact grade, stock condition and any heat treatment. Machined surfaces, polishing or a compatible protective treatment. Identify working interfaces, burr limits and required material records. Copper & brass Specify the alloy and standard for contacts or fittings. Polishing or compatible plating. Define contact areas, threads and post-plating dimensions. Engineering plastics Specify polymer grade, fillers and stock condition. Machined surfaces with agreed edge quality and cleaning. Include application conditions and the measurement state. Titanium is also listed in the milling scope. Specify its grade and condition for a separate tooling, finishing and availability review. Material and finish checklist CNC milling in practice The workshop sequence shows a part machined in two setups. Define how features from both setups relate on the drawing. The first milling setup in the workshop sequence. Establish the first features Choose stock and clamping faces so the initial pockets, bores and datum surfaces remain accessible. The same part in its second milling setup. Locate for the next setup Use defined locating features when turning the workpiece to machine another face. Two setups for one milled part Follow the same visible part between its first and second locating setups. Scope to confirm separately Do all features need five-axis machining? No. A part may use a three-axis setup, several setups or indexed positioning. The feature directions, contour and fixture clearance determine the route. Can you machine sharp internal corners? Specify the required corner geometry and any permitted radius. Tool access and internal corners need review; do not assume a standard milling tool can produce every sharp corner. ## /capabilities/cnc-turning-service/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：无。
- 当前H1：CNC Turning Services
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Fit the process to the drawing → h3: One axis, several working features → h3: Size and workholding inputs → h3: Use the feature drawing to settle the route → h2: Turned forms, different working interfaces → h3: Flanged turned component assemblies → h3: Rotary & cylindrical components → h3: Turned fittings, connectors & flanges → h2: Material and finish review → h2: Turning, secondary machining, finished features → h3: Turn the rotational features → h3: Machine the off-axis features → h2: Scope to confirm separately → h3: What sizes and tolerances can you machine? → h3: Can turned parts include cross-holes and flats? → h2: Request a CNC turning quote
- 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / CNC turning CNC Turning Services Shafts, sleeves and fittings built around a shared axis. CNC turning for shafts, bushings and fittings. Define the fits and threads on your drawing, with sealing faces and off-axis features marked. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Fit & acceptance Part forms Prepare RFQ email Fit the process to the drawing Cross-holes, flats or slots may need secondary machining. We plan these operations around their positions relative to the turned features. One axis, several working features Relate fitted diameters, shoulders and the bore to a common datum scheme. Show where threads, grooves and secondary holes must sit relative to those datums. Size and workholding inputs Stock size, overall dimensions, datum axis and access to internal grooves. Maximum size is reviewed per drawing. Feature / decision Specify on the drawing Agree before production Diameters & axial lengths Working diameters, bore depths, shoulder positions and mating fits. Agree dimensional measurement methods, acceptance limits and recorded results for diameters and axial relationships under the specified measurement conditions. Datum axis & runout Datum references, surfaces sharing an axis, assembly controls and off-axis features located from the turned datum axis. Agree the locating method and the runout or positional results to record. Threads & engagement Complete thread designation, length and mating connection. NPT, BSPT and metric are separate specifications. Confirm the appropriate thread gauge, engagement and acceptance requirements. Sealing surfaces & edges Surface texture, grooves, edge conditions and post-treatment dimensions. Agree dimensional and surface checks; define leak tests and application approvals separately. Plan the checks and approval stages Typical turned geometry. AI-generated illustration. Use the feature drawing to settle the route Shafts, sleeves, bushings and fittings. Live tooling adds flats and cross-holes; NPT, BSPT and metric threads are available. Identify critical features and permitted geometry changes. The concept explains the review; it is not an inspected part or setup record. Turned forms, different working interfaces Identify the rotational shape, then locate the features that need drilling, threading or milling. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Flanged turned component assemblies Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Flanged faces, coaxial bores, mounting holes and the supplied component scope. View part requirements Rotary & cylindrical components Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Turned forms with bores and off-axis features. View part requirements Turned fittings, connectors & flanges Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Threads and flanges with mating or sealing interfaces. View part requirements Material and finish review Specify grade, condition, finish and the final acceptance state together. These are discussion options; suitability and availability are confirmed for the drawing. Material / scope Specification Finish / treatment Confirm for this drawing Aluminum 6061, 7075; specify the temper. As-machined, polishing or anodizing. Identify fitted areas to protect and dimensions to check after anodizing. Stainless steel 303, 304 or 316L; specify the standard and condition. Fine-turned surfaces, polishing, passivation or electropolishing. Mark bearing and sealing surfaces; request any material or treatment records. Copper & brass Examples include C11000 copper and C36000 brass. Polishing or compatible protective plating. Define contact or seal areas, material restrictions and final dimensions after plating. Engineering plastics POM, PTFE or PEEK; specify the exact polymer grade. Machined surfaces with specified edge quality and cleaning. State conditioning or measurement-temperature requirements for fitted dimensions. Material and finish checklist Turning, secondary machining, finished features These original photographs show a turning setup and a separate secondary milling setup. They are setup references, not a complete trace of the same part or order. CNC turning at LZZ: workpiece, turret and coolant. Turn the rotational features The workpiece rotates while the tool forms diameters, shoulders, bores and grooves. Material, stock size and clamping access guide the setup. A turned component located for a subsequent milling operation. Machine the off-axis features Cross-holes, flats and slots may use live tooling or a subsequent milling setup. Their positions are controlled from the turned datums. Turning followed by milling Review a secondary milling setup and the datum information needed for a new order. Scope to confirm separately What sizes and tolerances can you machine? Send the finished dimensions, stock size, material and critical tolerances. Maximum turning size and achievable limits are reviewed against the drawing, workholding, tool access and inspection method. Can turned parts include cross-holes and flats? Yes. Depending on the geometry, these features may use live tooling or a secondary milling operation. Locate them relative to the turned datum axis and include access constraints. Request a CNC turning quote Datum axis, diameters and shoulders Thread standard and sealing arrangement Off-axis features and deburring access Material, quantity and inspection records Quantity, delivery destination and requested date Current drawing, model and revision Include mating-part information and the stock grade and condition. Specify the surface finish and quantity per release. If you need functional testing or a report, state the method and acceptance criteria for agreement. Prepare RFQ email Prepare a draft in your browser. Review it, attach drawings in your email application and send it yourself. Compare capability routes Review order and timing conditions

Specify threads, seal surfaces and post-plating dimensions. Other copper specifications Tellurium copper is listed in the service scope; specify its grade. Finish reviewed for the exact alloy. Include material restrictions, declarations and required records. C3600, C36000 and H62 are separate source designations and are not assumed equivalent. Confirm the requested standard and alloy. Material and finish checklist Contacts, fittings & connectors Electrical contacts and fluid fittings require different acceptance checks. Define the function alongside the part geometry. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Turned brass & copper components Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Threaded fitting forms. Review the thread and access Provide full thread designations, engagement and passage details. Locate seal or contact surfaces from the mating connection and specify burr limits. Protect the final interfaces Define plating, masking and dimensions after treatment. Agree contact, conductivity, thermal and pressure tests with separate methods and limits. Metal part collection Compare visible threaded and turned forms; the collection does not certify their alloy. Accept the part in its final specified state An electrical contact needs different checks from a fluid fitting. Choose the alloy for that function and state the required tests. Any electrical or fluid-system qualification is assessed separately for the application. Feature / decision Specify on the drawing Agree before production Alloy & restrictions Exact alloy designation, standard and any material restrictions. Confirm availability of requested declarations and material records. Threads & sealing faces Thread standard, engagement, seal geometry and internal passages. Agree thread gauges, dimensional measurement methods and burr checks for contact or sealing surfaces. Pressure tests need separate criteria. Contact & plated surfaces Contact area, plating specification and final dimensions. Define the post-plating dimensional records. Conductivity and thermal tests need agreed methods and limits. Agree the inspection records Scope to confirm separately Are C3600, C36000 and H62 interchangeable? No equivalence is assumed. Confirm the exact designation, applicable standard and any material restrictions before quotation. Are conductivity or pressure tests included? Request them explicitly with methods, limits and reporting requirements. A machined fitting or contact shape alone does not demonstrate functional qualification. Request a copper or brass quote Exact alloy designation and standard Electrical, thermal or sealing function Threads, contact faces and plating Final dimensions and any functional testing Quantity, delivery destination and requested date Current drawing, model and revision Include the alloy designation and finish or plating specification. - 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / Small production runs Low-Volume CNC Production Each part number. Each revision. Each release. There is no universal low-volume threshold. Geometry, workholding, material, finishing and inspection scope determine the practical batch size. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Define the release Single part and repeat batch. AI-generated illustration. Lock the requirements for every line item Use these fields for each part number. A mixed-part order still needs a separate controlled specification for every component. Part identity Part number, units and controlled model / drawing revision. Identify the controlling file if requirements conflict. Quantity and release Quantity for each line item, requested release dates and destination. Keep expected repeat demand or forecast separate from the order. Material and finish Exact grade, condition, stock form, treatment and approved alternatives for every part number. Appearance and packing Cosmetic faces, protected fits, edges, part identification and packaging requirements for each item. Shared interfaces Identify components that must fit together, their datums and the inspection results that control assembly. Approved material and finish alternatives must stay attached to the line item. A forecast is planning information; it does not replace a confirmed purchase quantity or release date. Agree the hold point before the first release First-article checks, sampling and approval responsibilities belong in the quotation and order. Before the first parts Approve the production revision, critical features, datums and acceptance limits. Agree measurement methods, report format and whether production waits for first-part approval. Review the result Use the agreed first-article measurements and sampling records. Keep part or batch identification and define nonconforming-part handling and deviation approval. Release or change Name the approver, record the release decision and authorize any changed specification. Repeat orders use the approved baseline and change-control process. Records to specify First-article and dimensional results In-process and final sampling Material and finishing traceability Nonconformance and deviation decisions Release dates and approval authority Inspection stages Order records Pilot builds & repeat part families A small batch may contain several part numbers, materials and revisions. Plan the requirements for each component and release. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Pilot-batch housing references Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Rows of small housing forms. Define the revision, quantity and shared interfaces for each part number. View part requirements Pilot-run component references Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Repeated turned forms. Define quantities and releases per part number. View part requirements Open aluminum enclosure references Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. A recessed enclosure with lid, gasket and mounting features. View part requirements Keep the repeat baseline and packing aligned Keep the approved part number, revision, grade, condition and finish with each release. Authorize changes before machining; review their effect on shared interfaces and sampling. Define protection for working surfaces, threads, cosmetic faces and vulnerable edges. Agree part identification, quantities per pack, required records and delivery responsibilities. Packing and shipping checklist Agree the packing reference Request a reference for the proposed packing: separated parts, interface protection, part / revision / quantity labels and the packing arrangement. Confirm these requirements for your order. Request a small-batch quote Part numbers, current revisions and quantities Release plan and change approval First-article, sampling and traceability records Repeat requirements, finish and packaging Quantity, delivery destination and requested date Current drawing, model and revision Give quantities per part and release, with the required documentation. State finish and packaging needs. Provide expected repeat demand as a forecast, with requested dates for confirmation. Agree commercial terms separately. ## /capabilities/prototype-machining/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：prototype-mobile.png、prototype-desktop.png。
- 当前H1：CNC Prototype Machining
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Fix the interfaces and the approval point → h2: Prototypes for assembly & design checks → h3: Billet aluminum engine covers → h3: Aluminum intercooler prototypes → h3: Throttle-body CAD reference → h2: If the prototype differs from production → h2: Record the next release decision → h3: Revise the design → h3: Plan a repeat release → h2: Submit a defined evaluation
- 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / Prototype machining CNC Prototype Machining What does this prototype need to answer? Define the fit, assembly, appearance or functional question for the current revision. Set the fixed interfaces, checks and approval responsibility before machining. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Define the evaluation and approval Part and drawing review. AI-generated illustration. Fix the interfaces and the approval point Keep the evaluation purpose, manufacturing inputs and reviewable result in one record. Stage Input Method and responsibility Reviewable result Define the question Current STEP or IGES model and controlled drawing revision; fit, assembly, appearance or defined functional objective. Mark fixed mating interfaces, mounting datums and clearances. Record open design decisions and approved material or finish alternatives. An agreed revision and evaluation scope, with the decisions the prototype can support. Make the agreed version Material, condition, finish, prototype quantity and critical interfaces. Agree measurements, locating fixtures, test procedures and acceptance limits before machining. Define separate pressure or vehicle tests and who performs them. The prototype and the dimensional or functional records agreed for the evaluation. Review and approve Measured interfaces, fit or test results and any remaining issues. Name the approver. Record acceptance, deviations and changes needed for the next revision. A reviewable approval or change record. A later prototype or batch requires its own released revision. Prototype approval checklist Prototypes for assembly & design checks Covers, cooling components and throttle bodies can support a development review when the evaluation question is defined. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Billet aluminum engine covers Original reference photograph of a curved cover and its visible tool-path texture. A contoured cover with interfaces for assembly checks. View part requirements Aluminum intercooler prototypes Original reference photograph of an intercooler form with its fins and end tanks. Intake and cooling-system interfaces for prototype fit checks. View part requirements Throttle-body CAD reference CAD reference · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Throttle-body bores and mounting interfaces for development checks. View part requirements Cover forms support fit and clearance discussion. Cooling prototypes also need joint and supplied-part scope; throttle-body references need bore and actuator-interface scope. These forms do not establish vehicle-performance results. If the prototype differs from production Use the production material, condition and finish, or approve alternatives that suit the evaluation. Record which conclusions cannot be carried over to the intended production part. Material / scope Specification Finish / treatment Confirm for this drawing Aluminum Specify alloy and temper; examples include 6061 and 7075. Deburring, polishing, bead blasting or anodizing. Mark protected fits and dimensions that apply after treatment. Steel & stainless steel Specify the exact grade, stock condition and any heat treatment. Machined surfaces, polishing or a compatible protective treatment. Identify working interfaces, burr limits and required material records. Copper & brass Specify the alloy and standard for contacts or fittings. Polishing or compatible plating. Define contact areas, threads and post-plating dimensions. Engineering plastics Specify polymer grade, fillers and stock condition. Machined surfaces with agreed edge quality and cleaning. Include application conditions and the measurement state. Specify the production material and finish, or approve alternatives for the evaluation. Record where the prototype differs from the intended production part. Material and finish checklist Record the next release decision Revise the design Record feedback, open issues and permitted changes. Identify and approve the next model / drawing revision before another prototype is machined. Plan a repeat release Keep prototype acceptance tied to its revision and evaluation scope. Agree the batch baseline, quantity, sampling and commercial order before repeat production. Plan the first batch Review responsibilities from quotation to release Submit a defined evaluation Fit, function, appearance or design question Current revision and fixed interfaces Material and finish needed for the evaluation Checks, records and approval responsibility Quantity, delivery destination and requested date Current drawing, model and revision Provide the model and key drawing dimensions, with the revision status. Give the prototype quantity and intended evaluation. Explain any planned next revision or later batch. Include your requested date for review and confirmation. Prepare RFQ email Prepare a draft in your browser. Review it, attach drawings in your email application and send it yourself. Compare capability routes Review order and timing conditions

## /capabilities/stainless-steel-cnc-parts/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：无。
- 当前H1：Stainless Steel CNC Machining
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Start with the specification → h2: Grade, condition and finishing → h2: Shafts, fittings & locking hardware → h3: Flanged turned component assemblies → h3: Rotary component & flange reference → h3: Indexing plungers & locking pins → h2: Plan the stainless machining sequence → h3: Confirm grade & condition → h3: Define turning & secondary features → h3: Check after the agreed treatment → h2: Accept the part in its final specified state → h2: Scope to confirm separately → h3: Can 304, 316L and 17-4PH be substituted? → h3: Do you machine locking hardware? → h2: Request a stainless steel quote
- 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / Stainless steel machining Stainless Steel CNC Machining Stainless steel shafts, fittings and locking hardware. Stainless steel shafts, fittings and locking hardware. Specify the material grade and condition, with thread and finish requirements. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Grade & finish Part forms Prepare RFQ email Start with the specification Define the exact grade, condition and finish for your drawing. A photograph does not establish the material or its properties. When the grade is unknown, send the application conditions for review. Grade, condition and finishing Specify grade, condition, finish and the final acceptance state together. These are discussion options; suitability and availability are confirmed for the drawing. Material / scope Specification Finish / treatment Confirm for this drawing 303 / 304 SUS303 and SUS304 are listed examples. Fine turning, polishing or specified passivation. Confirm grade, condition, thread requirements and material records. 316 / 316L Specify SUS316 or SUS316L explicitly. Polishing, passivation or electropolishing as specified. Identify sealing surfaces and post-treatment dimensions. 17-4PH Specify the required heat-treatment condition. Agree machining and surface-treatment sequence. Define final dimensions and the required material condition. Other listed stainless grades SUS430 and SUS440; specify the exact designation. Treatment reviewed against the selected grade. Review operating conditions and acceptance requirements. Material and finish checklist Shafts, fittings & locking hardware Working diameters, threads and sliding interfaces need a grade and condition selected for the service environment. Images identify visible forms; the approved drawing establishes material, tolerances and supply scope. Flanged turned component assemblies Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Flanged faces, coaxial bores, mounting holes and the supplied component scope. View part requirements Rotary component & flange reference Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. A rotary form with a separate flange and direction markings. Define bore axes, mounting pattern and supplied scope. View part requirements Indexing plungers & locking pins Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. Locking-pin fits and the interfaces of a spring-loaded assembly. View part requirements Plan the stainless machining sequence The material and final working surfaces guide the sequence. Source photographs show visible forms, rather than a controlled order history or verified material properties. Workholding inputs: Stock dimensions, datum axis, working diameters and shoulders. Confirm grade & condition Select the material for the operating environment and identify the required documentation. Define turning & secondary features Locate flats, holes, engraving and threads from the working diameters and shoulders. Check after the agreed treatment Specify the final measurement stage, burr limits and acceptance records. Accept the part in its final specified state Specify the stainless grade and condition for the operating environment. Include the surface treatment and dimensions required after treatment. The listed grades are alternatives, rather than one specification for every example. Feature / decision Specify on the drawing Agree before production Diameters & runout Datum axis, fitted diameters, shoulders and geometric controls. Agree the locating setup, measurement methods and recorded diameter or runout results. Threads & sliding features Thread designation, engagement, grooves, sealing or sliding surfaces and mating components. Confirm thread gauges, fit checks, burr limits and permitted handling marks. Treatment & documentation Grade, condition, heat treatment and surface-treatment specification. Define post-treatment dimensions and required material or process records. Agree the inspection records Scope to confirm separately Can 304, 316L and 17-4PH be substituted? They have different properties and conditions. Specify the selected grade for the service environment; any substitution needs your approval. Do you machine locking hardware? The catalog includes indexing plungers and locking-pin assemblies. Define the pin fits, mating interfaces and any separate functional requirements. Request a stainless steel quote Grade, condition and service environment Working diameters, datum axis and threads Passivation or electropolishing requirements Post-treatment dimensions and material records Quantity, delivery destination and requested date Current drawing, model and revision Send a drawing with the revision identified, plus the grade and condition. State the finish, quantity and mating-part requirements. For passivation or electropolishing, specify the treatment requirements and dimensions to check after treatment. Prepare RFQ email Prepare a draft in your browser. Review it, attach drawings in your email application and send it yourself. Compare capability routes Review order and timing conditions

## /capabilities/starting-with-a-sample/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：sample-mobile.png、sample-desktop.png。
- 当前H1：Machining from an Existing Sample
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Record the sample and the intended part → h3: CNC-machined casting housings → h2: Know what each stage will deliver → h2: Keep unknown material and treatment explicit → h2: Approve the first part before a repeat release → h2: Send the information available now
- 当前独有正文：Home / Capabilities / Starting with a sample Machining from an Existing Sample A sample can start the review. An approved specification starts production. Photographs support an initial discussion. Resolve the geometry, material, intended fit and acceptance requirements on an approved model and drawing before machining. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Reconstruction and drawing-approval timing are agreed separately. Record what to retain, resolve and approve AI-generated sample-to-drawing concept; not an approved drawing or reconstruction record. Record the sample and the intended part An existing housing can help establish shape and fit. Record wear, hidden features and intended changes before reconstructing the drawing. Retain Overall dimensions, working faces, mating parts and the features to keep. Resolve Wear, damage, hidden geometry, unknown material or treatment, and requested changes. Approve The intended fit, material, finish, model and drawing revision, and acceptance requirements for the new part. CNC-machined casting housings Source listing photograph · Original form retained; drawing and supplied scope require confirmation. A housing form for discussing replacement interfaces; it is not a reconstruction record for an order. View part requirements Know what each stage will deliver Agree the measurement and modeling scope, file formats and approval authority in the quotation. Stage Input Output Approval responsibility Sample review Photographs, overall dimensions, mating parts and available drawings; identify wear, damage and intended changes. A list of retained features, unknowns and measurements needed. Photographs support the initial review. Agree the review and measurement scope before reconstruction. Geometry measurement and reconstruction The sample and measurements or scanning needed for accessible and hidden geometry, including datum relationships. The agreed model and 2D / 3D drawing deliverables. Geometry alone does not establish tolerances, material, finish or the intended fit. Agree which files and formats are included in the quotation and who may approve them. Approved manufacturing definition Reviewed geometry, material, condition, finish, tolerances and acceptance requirements. The released model / drawing revision and a first-part acceptance plan. Approve the definition before machining; hold repeat production until the agreed first-part review is complete. Keep unknown material and treatment explicit Color, sample geometry and appearance do not identify a grade or treatment. Retain unknowns until the replacement specification and any substitutions are approved. Material / scope Specification Finish / treatment Confirm for this drawing Known material Provide the existing specification, grade and condition if available. Record the known finish and any changes required. Confirm the specification on the approved drawing. Unknown material Mark unknown grades or treatments explicitly. Do not identify a treatment from appearance alone. Agree how material and finish requirements will be established. Replacement requirements Specify the intended application and mating components. Define cosmetic and working surfaces separately. Approve substitutions and acceptance requirements before machining. Material and finish checklist Approve the first part before a repeat release Approve the manufacturing definition before machining. Sample condition, hidden features and intended fit need to be resolved with the drawing. Feature / decision Specify on the drawing Agree before production Sample condition Worn or damaged areas, retained features and requested changes. Agree which geometry represents the intended part rather than sample wear. Hidden features & fit Internal measurements, mating parts, bores, threads and sealing surfaces. Define how inaccessible geometry and datum relationships will be established. Approved deliverables Model and drawing revision, material, finish, tolerances and file formats. Identify who approves the documents and which files the quotation includes. First-part review Quantity, fit or functional checks, records and acceptance limits. Agree the measurement or functional-test method, limits, required records and authorized first-part approver before releasing repeat production. Agree the inspection records Send the information available now Sample photographs, dimensions and mating parts Known material, finish and current drawings Wear, damage and intended changes Required model and drawing deliverables First-part quantity and acceptance checks Repeat demand, destination and requested date Prepare RFQ email Prepare a draft in your browser. Review it, attach drawings in your email application and send it yourself. Compare capability routes Review order and timing conditions

## /content-layout-demo-20261010/

- 类别：preview；本轮当前DOM：未记录；视觉截图：无。
- 当前H1：
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: 改造前 当前主站 → h2: 改造后 独立样板 → h2: 理解企业 → 找到加工路线 → 查看实拍证据 → 准备询价
- 当前独有正文：保留现有 Inter 字体、品牌红、业务信息与 URL。先确认三个样板，再扩展到全站。“改造前”加载当前原页；“改造后”加载独立 Demo。页面可滚动、展开和点击，询价沿用现有邮件草稿流程。首页分类 · 塑料加工详情 · 塑料零件视口Desktop · 1440pxMobile · 390px比较前后并排只看改造后只看改造前改造前 当前主站全尺寸打开 ↗改造后 独立样板全尺寸打开 ↗客户阅读与转化路径理解企业 → 找到加工路线 → 查看实拍证据 → 准备询价真实车间承担企业身份表达；四类产品帮助客户识别供货范围。主入口与辅助材料索引不再竞争。KEEP 保留定位、三个原精选零件、五项能力、四类材料、公司事实、工厂/检验照片、三项研究与邮件草稿流程。REORDER 重排原始车间照片进入首屏；新增已有塑料产品入口。先看产品，再按几何选择工艺。MERGE 合并能力与材料共用一个选型区域；工厂和质量证据成为一个连贯模块。SHORTEN / MOVE 缩短、迁移缩短首屏及重复介绍；完整工厂、质量及研究细节继续由原页面承接，原素材保留。整站最值得调整的 10 个问题 按改造收益排序首页整屏背景先于车间与产品车间与产品提前，首屏缩短。详情手机长摘要挤掉主图标题后先图像，再呈现技术表与验收条件。塑料分类实物片段出现过晚将现有实物与视频前移为一个示例模块。材料与制造信息分散重复合并材料列表，保留独有选项及牌号限制。首页工艺、材料和 CTA 主次不清五项工艺主入口，材料辅助；加入现有塑料零件。首页、Factory、About 反复突出同组事实和流程页面分工；详细现场和流程由现有专页承接。About 企业文件入口埋在背景故事后身份与文件前移，历史、应用、人物降权。加工研究图片前有多层介绍与目录图片与具体加工问题提前，来源及完整技术说明保留。分类、详情、FAQ 结尾出口竞争一个主行动、两个相关资料，其余渐进展示。Contact 手机版询价区域出现在长联系区之后询价入口与联系渠道靠近，缩短准备图纸后的行动路径。本轮只新增三个样板及对比页，没有替换主站模板、删除技术内容或添加企业能力证明。Factory、About、案例、Contact 等建议等待样板确认后再实施。

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：factory-mobile.png、factory-desktop.png。
- 当前H1：Factory & quality
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Put the scale in context → h2: Check the equipment against the part → h3: Milling centers → h3: Lathes & turn-mill equipment → h3: Multi-axis machining → h3: Inputs for equipment fit → h2: Agree the checks and approval stages → h2: Match the method to the feature → h2: Specify the records and verify the supplier documents → h3: Inspection and order records → h3: ISO 9001 certification → h2: Define the handover with the parts → h3: Confirm the packing arrangement → h2: Start the supplier and part review
- 当前独有正文：Home / Factory & quality Factory & quality Review the workshop, the checks and the records. Custom CNC machining in Hangzhou for prototypes and small production runs. Match the visible setup references and agreed inspection plan to the requirements of your part. Workshop Equipment fit Inspection Records Delivery Original LZZ workshop photograph, Hangzhou. Visible machine rows and working space support a site review; the photograph does not verify current equipment counts. Put the scale in context Company facts follow the supplied business information. Request a current equipment list and project-specific capacity review where your supplier assessment needs them. Established / location 2017 · Hangzhou, China Production facility 1,500 m² workshop Equipment 40+ machining and supporting units Team experience More than 15 years of CNC machining experience Reference capacity About 100,000 small parts per month. Output varies with geometry, material, finishing and the mix of orders. The reporting period, shifts, part mix and current supporting records still need confirmation. Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7–14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including finishing. Shipping time is additional. Confirm the schedule for your quantity and requirements in the quotation. Operators and machines on the LZZ production floor. The November 2025 source archive is not a verification of current headcount, area or output. Check the equipment against the part Bores, pockets, threads and angled faces need different access and holding arrangements. These are original setup references. Milling centers Milling an enclosure in the LZZ workshop. Pockets, flat faces, bores and multi-face features. Housings, instrument blocks and brackets. Explore CNC milling Lathes & turn-mill equipment A component held for turning. Rotational parts, threads and features combining turning and milling. Shafts, sleeves, fittings and flanges. Explore CNC turning Multi-axis machining A wider view of the multi-axis setup. Angled faces, curved surfaces and access from several directions. Multi-face components and complex machined forms. Explore 5-axis machining Inputs for equipment fit Send finished-part and stock dimensions, weight and clamping restrictions. Review machine travel, load, tool access, fixture clearance and protected surfaces for the drawing. Maximum sizes are not published here. Request the relevant machine models and specifications for qualification. Equipment count includes supporting units; a photograph does not establish machine range or simultaneous five-axis motion. Agree the checks and approval stages The drawing defines critical features, datums and acceptance limits. Specify sampling, measurement methods and required records before machining. Stage What is checked Agree before the stage Drawing review Agree critical features, datums, acceptance limits and the required records. Confirm the drawing revision and which features must be recorded. First article Check the initial part before the production run. Agree in advance if production must wait for your sample approval. In-process checks Inspect agreed features during machining. Define check points, sampling and the handling of deviations. Final inspection Verify the finished parts against the agreed drawing and order. Identify checks before or after finishing and the final release records. Define the critical drawing features Original coordinate measuring machine photograph. It shows the equipment and setting; current range, calibration, accuracy and report availability require separate confirmation. Match the method to the feature Consistent support, drawing datums and the measurement stage matter alongside instrument selection. Agree whether the dimensions apply before or after finishing. Feature Method Agree for the drawing Positions & geometric features Coordinate measuring machine (CMM) Drawing datums, feature limits, support and measurement strategy. Heights & linear dimensions Height gauges The reference surface, setup and accessible features. Suitable profiles Optical projectors The profile, reference and measurement orientation. Threads, surface finish & special tests Confirm the method in your quotation Specify thread, roughness, coating-thickness or hardness checks and records. AI-generated inspection concept. The visible contact explains the method; it is not an LZZ inspection record or instrument qualification. Request the instrument range and current calibration evidence relevant to the specified features. Thread gauging, roughness, coating-thickness, hardness and other special methods and records must be confirmed in the quotation. Specify the records and verify the supplier documents Each record needs a defined scope and reference to the part, drawing revision or batch. Confirm current availability and the required document package for the order. Inspection and order records Record Content Reference and agreement Dimensional & first-article reports Measured features, datums, limits, methods and results. Drawing revision, part or batch reference, sampling and review details. Material records Grade, condition and the traceability needed for the part. Document type and availability confirmed in the quotation. Finishing documents Finishing process, special tests and required records. Part or batch reference and any checks after treatment. Surface & batch checks Cosmetic faces, masking, edges and sampling. Acceptance criteria and who approves any deviation. Request a relevant sample inspection report Download acceptance checklist ISO 9001 certification LZZ is ISO 9001 certified. Request the current certificate and scope for your supplier review. Legal entity Hangzhou Lizhizao Precision Machinery Co., Ltd. Certificate Current certificate copy, number and issuing certification body. Validity & scope Issue and expiry dates, covered site and certified activities. Verification Verification method, relevant inspection records and calibration evidence. Request the ISO certificate Certificate version, number, issuing body, validity and scope are completed from the authorized current certificate. Company recognition and teaching documents are described separately in the company profile. Company recognition documents Define the handover with the parts After final release, connect interface protection, identification and agreed records to the packing and delivery responsibilities in the quotation. Protect sealing faces, threads, cosmetic surfaces and vulnerable features. Identify part number, drawing revision and quantity for each pack. Include the agreed dimensional, material and finishing records. Confirm packing arrangements, shipment documents, destination and delivery responsibilities. Drawing, approval and delivery guide Packing and shipping checklist Confirm the packing arrangement Request a relevant original reference showing individual separation, interface protection, identification fields and the completed packing arrangement for the proposed part. Agree your protection and packing requirements in the quotation. Historical large-part shipment photographs do not define this order's packing. Start the supplier and part review STEP model and controlled 2D drawing with part number, units and revision The controlling document if model and drawing requirements conflict Material grade and condition, finish, quantity and protected surfaces Critical features, datums, tolerances and acceptance limits First-article hold points, sampling, methods, reports and approval responsibility Delivery destination, requested date, packing and handover requirements Prepare RFQ email Prepare the draft in your browser. Review it, attach the drawing package in your email application and send it yourself. Review project responsibilities

- 当前独有正文：独立本地预览 · 正式首页未覆盖相同的业务内容，三种阅读体验。用产品、加工能力和真实资料建立采购路径。三版沿用 Inter 字体与现有业务 URL，差异来自构图、图片组织、字重和页面节奏。内容基线与素材交接说明A精密产品目录主参考 14 号白底产品特写 / 克制字重 / 清晰目录网格先看零件，再进入加工能力和材料。适合快速选型、比较与询价。打开完整首页图片处理与已知限制叶轮原图经 ImageGen 编辑为工作室概念主视觉；产品列表保留现有主体图。主视觉存在重绘细节差异，不作为型号或规格依据。1440px 桌面全页390px 手机全页手机首屏产品区细节质量区细节询价区细节B深色材质展陈主参考 2 号深色材质首屏 / 不对称图组 / 明暗章节切换用铸件结构形成视觉记忆，产品之后前置车间与检测证据。打开完整首页图片处理与已知限制壳体原图经 ImageGen 编辑，左侧背景融合；车间、检测与加工图沿用原资料。主视觉的孔口与纹理有重绘差异；目录图仍使用原有素材。1440px 桌面全页390px 手机全页手机首屏产品区细节质量区细节询价区细节C开阔工程画册主参考 6 号横向产品组合 / 冷蓝灰空间 / 错落图片与列表能力入口前置，以工艺目录引导选型，应用研究采用大图与说明并列。打开完整首页图片处理与已知限制ImageGen 仅制作摄影棚背景，三件产品使用原透明主体等比例合成；另做手机构图。组合图不是同场实拍，不代表产品相对尺寸。1440px 桌面全页390px 手机全页手机首屏产品区细节质量区细节询价区细节三版保持相同的信息覆盖按图定制与原型/小批量定位 · 原首页三个产品入口及塑料零件补充入口 · 五类加工能力 · 四类材料 · 起订量与生产时间及其条件 · 车间、检测与企业资料 · 三个加工研究 · 合作流程 · NDA 提示与询价准备。询价演示仅在当前页面生成摘要，不发送邮件、不保存数据、不连接 CRM。普通内容链接会进入现有网站。参考图中无法辨认的字体、交互和手机排版没有假定为已知；本轮使用现有 Inter，响应式布局独立设计。ISO 证书有效期、检测校准与最新供货条件仍需企业确认。查看 1–16 号参考图对照原文件为哈希名，没有可确认的编号。以下按文件名升序编号，本轮所有参考编号均以此为准。119654eaf27e673970f9af6f982315dc5.jpg21a0c9e38c1887dc93b9c44f23226a01c.jpg325e4d72829c7e58266e17471112ceb46.jpg436d253fc7d026739d9c579e0593259a3.jpg544c204cd93595d9709e4648e222ffd25.jpg655d66aef156f9cea08c5d5f15b0170ea.jpg75e5caf3b8ec3bc2fb11b69527a583bee.jpg87f7f4618bdcb631a088dedcac462dfa9.jpg999c50ae2bdf58a23f954b75deb28b6e3.jpg109f8630211fdbd619430dd45515d57831.jpg11a5b783aeaf264ac7c18d92cab7c6778c.jpg12c5644b7860a9b908ba40715f517e85b2.jpg13c998d0104e83d5968f7a45c22610530e.jpg14e70a7ae069eff086411b78a9ed979f77.jpg15e934b9bd712209dae1fdd69407883e5b.jpg16fdf4791cfcc08095f453710553b8c5d3.jpg

## /privacy/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：privacy-desktop.png、privacy-mobile.png。
- 当前H1：Privacy and site information
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Your inquiry → h2: Saved drafts → h2: Cookies and external services → h2: Product and company information → h2: Contact
- 当前独有正文：Privacy and site information How inquiry drafts are stored and how to use the product information on this site. Your inquiry The inquiry form creates an email draft in your browser. It does not send your information to LZZ or upload drawings. To contact us, open the draft in your email app, add your attachments and send it yourself. Saved drafts Your input is kept in this browser tab so you can continue after a refresh. The draft is associated with the part or topic used when you entered the form. Changing the product or service inside the form updates that same draft; it does not create a separate saved copy. These details are not sent to LZZ or saved to an account. Select “Clear saved input” to remove the draft for the current entry context, including older copies associated with that entry. A browser that restores an earlier session may also restore your draft. Clear your input when using a shared device. If storage is blocked or full, you can still prepare a draft while the page is open, then choose “Save draft” to keep a text copy. Cookies and external services This site does not use analytics or advertising cookies. Your email provider, WhatsApp and other linked websites have their own privacy policies. Product and company information Product pages describe example parts and the information needed to quote a similar component. They are not independently verified specifications for the pictured parts. Your drawing and quotation define the material and tolerances, with inspection and commercial terms confirmed for your order. Original catalog records are retained in our archive. The engineering guides help you prepare an inquiry. They contain no customer inspection results and do not establish suitability for a particular application. English summaries explain the company documents; contact LZZ to verify their current validity. Inspection planning is agreed for your order. Contact Hangzhou Lizhizao Precision Machinery Co., Ltd. For questions about this site, contact roger@lzz-cnc.com Updated 15 October 2026.
