## /resources/rfq-checklist/

- 类别：business；本轮当前DOM：已读；视觉截图：rfq-desktop.png、rfq-mobile.png。
- 当前H1：CNC machining RFQ checklist
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: Files → h2: Material & finish → h2: Quantity → h2: Critical features → h2: Delivery → h2: Open decisions → h2: Drawings and inspection requirements → h2: Material and final surface → h2: Quantity and delivery → h2: Copy this brief into your inquiry → h3: Keep the acceptance requirements with the order → h2: Next decision → h3: Use the prepared information in an RFQ
- 当前独有正文：Home / Resources / Ordering guide / CNC machining RFQ checklist CNC machining RFQ checklist Prepare your files with the checklist, then copy the inquiry brief. Mark any specifications that still need to be agreed. Files Controlled model, drawing and revision. Material & finish Grade, condition and final surface. Quantity Initial and repeat requirements. Critical features Working features and acceptance. Delivery Destination, timing and shipping basis. Open decisions Unresolved requirements and alternatives. Use the inquiry brief Drawings and inspection requirements Drawing files and revision Send the STEP model and a 2D drawing marked with the part number, units and revision. If the files disagree, tell us which takes precedence. For multi-axis parts, include the overall part and stock dimensions, plus their weights if known. Critical features Mark fits, datums, tolerances, threads and sealing faces. State whether dimensions apply before or after finishing. Inspection reports Tell us which report format you need. Before ordering, confirm the measurement methods and instrument range with us, along with the calibration evidence and records available. Working from a sample Tell us what you want to retain or change from the sample so we can agree the measurement and drawing work. Material and treatment requirements that cannot be established from the sample need to be specified separately. Inspection methods and records Material and final surface Material Specify the exact grade or functional requirement, material records and permitted substitutes. Finish State treatment, appearance, masked areas and edge conditions. Include applicable standards, revisions, scope and required certificates. Specify material and finish Quantity and delivery Orders start at 1 piece. Production takes 7 to 14 business days after drawing and order confirmation, including surface finishing. Transit time is additional. Order quantities Initial quantity per part number and expected repeat pattern, if known. Destination and timing Required arrival date, destination postcode and any decision or response deadline. Packing and responsibility Special protection or labels, shipping basis and responsibility for customs, duties and taxes. Packing and shipping requirements Copy this brief into your inquiry Complete the fields you know and mark open decisions for review. If you need an NDA, request it before sharing confidential drawings. Part number and drawing revision: Units; attached 3D model and 2D drawing: Material grade and condition: Finish, masked areas and cosmetic faces: Initial quantity; expected repeat quantity: Critical features and acceptance limits: Inspection records or certificates required: Delivery destination and required arrival date: Packing needs and requested shipping basis: Open questions or permitted alternatives: Identify which document controls if the drawing and model disagree. Ask for open requirements and proposed alternatives to be resolved in the quote. Copy inquiry brief Download brief Keep the acceptance requirements with the order The acceptance worksheet is blank. Use it to agree requirements; it is not an inspection report or a record of completed checks. Download acceptance worksheet Next decision Drawing requirements & critical features Define datums, fits, working surfaces and measurable acceptance. Compare quotations Check scope, exclusions and delivery terms on the same basis. Use the prepared information in an RFQ Bring the completed brief and any open decisions into one inquiry. The website prepares an email draft. Review it, attach drawings and send it from your email app. Prepare RFQ email

## /visual-directions-20261014/

- 类别：preview；本轮当前DOM：未记录；视觉截图：无。
- 当前H1：同样的业务，三种视觉表达。
- 当前模块（原序）：h2: 简约精修 → h2: 高端品牌 → h2: 图片主导 → h2: 本次设计调整
- 当前独有正文：首页视觉方向 · 独立本地预览同样的业务，三种视觉表达。三版保留相同的产品、能力、材料、工厂与质量信息、加工研究、采购指南及询价条件。选择方向后再统一其他页面。正式首页、导航和业务 URL 未改动。A简约精修Quiet refinement保留原有结构。暖灰产品区、浅灰绿能力区与深色询价区，让同一套内容有清晰的轻重变化。原有深色摄影首屏 / 四列产品 / 温暖背景层次打开完整首页桌面全页截图手机全页截图B高端品牌Material & craft石墨色首屏与叶轮大特写，配合宽幅工厂照片、错落研究模块和克制的衬线大标题。深色棚拍主视觉 / 双列产品陈列 / 品牌式图文节奏打开完整首页桌面全页截图手机全页截图C图片主导Parts in focus将价值主张与图片分开，大幅横向加工画面和不等比例产品图，缩短视觉理解路径。宽幅加工图片 / 非等距产品网格 / 简洁无衬线标题打开完整首页桌面全页截图手机全页截图本次设计调整保留当前 Inter / Georgia 字体、品牌红和 LZZ 标识。区别来自图片尺度、背景明度、版式与标题表现，而非更换业务定位。B 版叶轮主视觉基于现有图片通过 ImageGen 调整背景和光线，仅用于版式展示；工厂、检验设备和加工研究沿用现有素材。没有新造企业、认证或客户事实。页面内的产品、指南和询价链接进入现有页面，本次只改独立 Demo。询价仍为准备邮件草稿，不会由 Demo 自动发送。
